<analysis>**original_problem_statement:** The user wants to finalize a DeployVPS application. The initial core requirement was to modify the **redeploy** functionality to preserve the existing MongoDB database. Over time, the scope expanded significantly to include Apache2 support, a new SEO-optimized landing page, an improved deployment log viewer, security hardening, robust resource cleanup, a responsive UI during long operations, and fixing specific deployment bugs. More recently, the user requested a comprehensive MongoDB backup/restore/import system, intelligent conflict-avoiding port selection for new deployments, and full internationalization (multi-language support) for the application.

- **PRODUCT REQUIREMENTS:**
  - Preserve MongoDB data on redeploy.
  - Support for Apache2 servers (in addition to Nginx).
  - An attractive, SEO-optimized public landing page.
  - A non-blocking UI during deployments, with the ability to cancel an in-progress deployment.
  - Robust deployment deletion that cleans up all associated resources.
  - A comprehensive MongoDB backup system: manual creation, listing, downloading, restoring, deleting, and importing external backups.
  - An intelligent New Deployment form that automatically suggests available ports to prevent conflicts.
  - Full application translation into Portuguese, English, and Spanish.
  - Automatic language detection based on the user's IP address.
  - An admin dashboard with analytics on site usage and conversions.

**User's preferred language**: Portuguese. **The next agent MUST respond in Portuguese.**

**what currently exists?**
A full-stack (React/FastAPI/MongoDB) application, DeployVPS, that automates deploying web apps from GitHub to a user's VPS. It supports Nginx/Apache2, has an SEO-optimized landing page, user auth, and a dashboard for managing deployments.

Recent major additions include:
- A full MongoDB backup/restore/import system accessible from the deployment details page.
- An intelligent New Deployment form that asks for a single Base Port and automatically finds available ports on the VPS for the frontend, backend, and database to prevent conflicts.
- A multi-language system (i18n) using React Context. The , , , , and  pages are already translated.

**Last working item**:
- **Last item agent was working:** The agent was in the middle of a large, multi-part feature request from the user:
    1.  Translate all remaining pages.
    2.  Implement automatic language detection via the user's IP.
    3.  Add an analytics section to the Admin dashboard.
    The agent began by adding new translation keys to  and modifying  to include the logic for fetching location data from an IP geolocation API ().
- **Status:** IN PROGRESS
- **Agent Testing Done:** N
- **Which testing method agent to use?** Frontend testing agent. After completing the feature, use the testing agent to verify that all pages translate correctly, the language defaults based on a simulated IP address, and the new analytics page displays as expected.
- **User Testing Done:** N

**Completed work in this session**
- **Bug Fix: Reforçar Segurança**: Fixed a bug where the security hardening feature would repeatedly try to install already-active services like Fail2ban.
- **Deployment Flow UX:**
  - Implemented a Cancel Deploy button and backend logic to terminate a running deployment.
  - Added a confirmation prompt to prevent users from accidentally navigating away from the details page during an active deployment.
- **MongoDB Backup/Import/Restore:** Implemented a complete system accessible from the UI to create, list, download, restore, delete, and **import** database backups.
- **Bug Fix:  Deployment**: Resolved a critical deployment bug caused by a port conflict, where MongoDB was incorrectly assigned the same port as the backend. The agent corrected the container and database configurations, and the project now deploys successfully.
- **VPS Cleanup**: Implemented and ran a script to reclaim over 2GB of disk space on the VPS by removing orphaned Docker resources.
- **Intelligent Port Selection**: Reworked the New Deployment form to use a single Base Port, automatically checking the VPS for available ports and suggesting a conflict-free set for the frontend, backend, and database.
- **Multi-Language Foundation**: Established the i18n system using React Context and translated the , , , , and  pages into Portuguese, English, and Spanish.
